<span class="ct-node-label">Better Habits</span>
<span class="ct-node-title-full">What Actually Makes Self Improvement Stick?</span>
<span class="ct-node-summary">Self-improvement that works is usually less glamorous than self-help marketing suggests. The strongest evidence does not point to a particular morning routine, personality type or motivational philosophy.</span>
</span>

<span class="ct-node-label">Accountability</span>
<span class="ct-node-title-full">Does Telling Someone Else Help You Follow Through?</span>
<span class="ct-node-summary">Reporting progress to another person can make monitoring more consequential and may strengthen follow-through.</span>
</span>

<span class="ct-node-content">
<span class="ct-node-label">Goals vs Progress</span>
<span class="ct-node-title-full">Should You Tell People Your Goals or Your Progress?</span>
<span class="ct-node-summary">Announcing an aspiration and reporting completed work are different social acts, and research gives good reason not to treat them as interchangeable.</span>
</span>
</a>

<span class="ct-node-content">
<span class="ct-node-label">Behaviour Reports</span>
<span class="ct-node-title-full">What Should You Actually Report for Accountability?</span>
<span class="ct-node-summary">Accountability becomes clearer when another person sees whether specific planned actions happened, rather than hearing a general self-assessment.</span>

<span class="ct-node-title-full">When Does Accountability Become Too Much Pressure?</span>
<span class="ct-node-summary">Reporting can stop helping when judgement, pressure or surveillance makes people hide failures, avoid check-ins or focus on pleasing the observer.</span>

<span class="ct-node-title-full">Do Self Improvement Studies Prove Why Their Techniques Work?</span>

<span class="ct-node-summary">Linking a behaviour to a recurring routine can work about as well as using a fixed time, provided the cue is dependable and the action is repeated.</span>
</span>
</a>

<span class="ct-node-title-full">When Do Environmental Reminders Stop Getting Noticed?</span>
<span class="ct-node-summary">Adding more reminders is not always better because prompts that are vague, poorly placed, or constantly visible can become easy to overlook.</span>
</span>
